# BUILD_LOG

## Current state
- Repo: github.com/NamanJain-Engineer/tandem (private). Build kit in `build-kit/`; plan in `docs/PLAN.md`.
- Milestone: **M0–M14 are done** on `product-m0-m5`, and so are M15's six features. M9 was built in parallel on `m9-files-git-browser` (pull request #4), M10 on `m10-teammates-gateway` (pull request #5), M12 on `m12-plugins-approvals` (pull request #34), M8 on `m8-dictate-onboarding` (pull request #37), M11 on `m11-tasks-routines` (pull request #36, with M16's F-RT-05) and M14 on `m14-remote-ssh-wsl` (pull request #6), all merged 10 October 2026. 139 of 152 features are done: all 65 P0s and every P1, with F-DI-05 (cloud speech-to-text, D-516) and F-DI-10 (Enhance and Polish, D-517) finishing M13. The 13 left are M16's P2 extras. Next on their own branches: M8's session closes Dictate's open items; M10's F-AG-10, F-AG-13 and teammates in Find work wait on its go-ahead; M11's makes the early-access copy honest and bundles the tasks server; M15's non-functional checks, M16C (phone companion and relay) and M17 (extras beyond the spec) are in flight. The coordinating session merges each into `product-m0-m5`. `docs/REMAINING.md` lists every feature left. M7 is done (tag `m7`, local; 60/60 E2E spec files at the time; 65/65 with M9's specs). **Deployed:** vibezeo.com (`main`) serves 0.4.0 (see Release below); Dictate, tasks and routines, plugins and SSH and WSL workspaces reach the site and a download with the next release. Releases are built locally (`TANDEM_HOSTED=0 bash scripts/release-local.sh --dry-run`) and served from vibezeo.com/downloads with SHA256SUMS. The Release workflow (D-105 `early-access` channel, public `tandem-releases` repository, `RELEASES_TOKEN`) waits for Actions minutes, which reset about November 1; the owner chose not to pay for more. CI runs macOS and Windows jobs only on pushes to `main` and manual runs; pull requests run Linux only. Tags `m0`–`m7` are local only. The public site at vibezeo.com deploys from `main` (D-102, `docs/DEPLOY_SITE.md`).
- Release: **0.4.0 is live on vibezeo.com** (pull requests #32 and #33; deployed 10 October 2026; OPERATING §7 checks passed: home, demo, docs, changelog, llms.txt, openapi.json and site-info answer, the download matches SHA256SUMS, and the live demo reads v0.4.0 with no console errors) (Linux .deb, `TANDEM_HOSTED=0`, built with `bash scripts/release-local.sh --dry-run`; `Vibezeo_0.4.0_amd64.deb`, SHA-256 `b6ea61eb…cbb679`). It adds teammates (M10), Find work, Your data (export and delete), shortcut rebinding, the usage page and What's new, and fixes the rail's OFFLINE label; the CHANGELOG section lists only what the download does (What's new first shows on the update after this one, D-133). Smoke-tested offline from the AppImage: welcome says Free plan, setup detects the 11 engines, the status bar reads v0.4.0, the rail says FREE, Agent mode opens on Lead, and Find work searches. Before it: full E2E 81/82 (the 82nd, E2E-M16-5's light-theme axe sweep, was a theme-transition race, fixed by D-815) and the gate. The site's figures (10 milestones, 1,917 automated tests: 919 Rust and 998 TypeScript, 82 E2E spec files, 11 engines), the home page's features and roadmap, and the Agent mode, privacy and shortcuts docs match it; the demo reads its version from `apps/desktop/package.json`. Deploying is merging `product-m0-m5` into `main` (OPERATING §7). 0.3.0 went live earlier the same day (pull requests #18, #20 and #16). For the next release's notes: the status bar no longer claims Online in an early-access build (D-816).
- M16 Conductor voice API (F-BE-08, and the API halves of F-CN-02 and F-CN-05) is on `product-m0-m5` (pull request #30, built on `m16-voice-api`); see "M16 · F-BE-08 Conductor voice API". The desktop's Conductor client (F-CN-01…04, F-CN-08: calls, the local tool executor, the HUD, the call log) is on `m16a-conductor`; see "M16 · 16A Conductor core".
- Layout matches DECISIONS: 10 crates under `crates/`, Tauri crate `apps/desktop/src-tauri` (lib `tandem_app`, bin `tandem`), `apps/{api,web}`, `packages/{ui,protocol}`.
- Environment: WebKitGTK, webkit2gtk-driver, xvfb, tauri-driver, dbus, xclip, openbox, cargo-audit and a MinGW cross compiler (`cargo check --target x86_64-pc-windows-gnu`) are installed; `dockerd` must be started by hand in a fresh container (`dockerd &`), then `pnpm infra:up`. When the disk fills, run `python3 scripts/agent/prune-target.py`, delete `target/debug/incremental`, and delete old copies of the invariant test binaries (`target/debug/deps/rule_*`, about 550 MB each, one per threat-model rule per build); gating with `CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0` keeps the 3–4 GB incremental cache from coming back. A full disk shows up as a linker "Bus error" (signal 7).
- Gate: `pnpm gate` (lint, vitest, cargo test, desktop build, clippy, fmt, protocol drift) is green. Stage new files first: the drift check diffs the working tree against the index. `gen:protocol` also writes `packages/protocol/shortcuts.json`.
- Desktop E2E: `dbus-run-session -- xvfb-run -a -s "-screen 0 1920x1080x24" pnpm --filter desktop e2e` runs the real Tauri binary via tauri-driver (starts API + web if needed, openbox for WM behaviour, scripted Chromium for sign-in and checkout). 60/60 spec files pass (about 10 minutes). Pass one glob to `--spec` (`m7-{8,10}-*`): the runner keeps only the first `--spec`. Restore specs crash the app with `pkill -9` and relaunch; they sign the same account in again because the test keychain lives in memory. The run starts `scripts/mock-update-feed.ts` on :8790 (scenario `none`). `--suite startup` needs `gnome-keyring` (D-101). `TANDEM_E2E_SKIP_BUILD=1` reuses the last build; `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1` rewrites the TUI goldens (review them). zsh, fish, vim, htop and less must be installed (`apt-get install zsh fish htop`).
- API tests need `pnpm infra:up` (they create `tandem_test`). Web E2E: `PLAYWRIGHT_CHROMIUM=/opt/pw-browsers/chromium pnpm --filter web test:e2e` after `db:migrate`, `db:seed`, `keys:dev`.
- Release harness check: `bash scripts/check-release-harness.sh` (release build, asserts no `test_*` commands in the binary).
- Open issues: none blocking. The Free plan's `chatEngines` limit is enforced (D-127). `pnpm gen:docs` writes the docs' generated sources (shortcuts and settings JSON); `gen:protocol` runs it too. The Linux single-instance plugin needs a DBus session bus (E2E runs under `dbus-run-session`).

## M6 checklist (MILESTONES M6)
- [x] 1. `tandem-git` base: `Git` CLI runner (hardened reads per D-104: no fsmonitor, hooks, credential helper, pager, ssh/askpass, repo filter drivers, external diff or textconv; scrubbed `GIT_*` env, `LC_ALL=C`; writes use the user's config minus fsmonitor), `Repo` reads through libgit2 (status with staged / unstaged / untracked / conflicted, upstream ahead/behind, no-commit repos, subfolder prefix; head; branches; diff vs a base commit incl. untracked and binary, 512 KiB per-file cap; ancestry; index conflicts), `LaneManager` (create from HEAD under `<main root>/.tandem/worktrees/<slug>` on `tandem/<slug>`, remove with Dirty unless a `ForceConfirm` built from a `LocalReview`, `merge --no-ff --no-edit` with a conflict report and no commit, sweep = prune + merged lanes idle >7 days), `ensure_excluded` (`.tandem/` once in `info/exclude`), `slugify` + `dedupe`; `tandem_core::origin` (`Origin`, `LocalReview`); `invariant_rule_6_read_ops_no_repo_helpers` (traps proven live first) and `invariant_rule_5_local_review_origin`
- [x] 2. Lanes F-CD-15 (child workspaces, lane menu, sweep notification). Backend done: worktrees are child `workspaces` rows (`parent_id`, `lane_branch`, `lane_base`; outside the Free workspace limit; a removed row at the same folder is reused) with one tab each; `lane_create` / `lane_diff` / `lane_merge` / `lane_create_pr` / `lane_remove` / `lanes_sweep` with tests for AC1–3 in `services/lanes.rs`; forced removal needs the main window plus a native "Discard {branch}?" confirm (`TANDEM_FAKE_CONFIRM` in debug); the sweep runs at startup and every 6 h and raises one in-app "{n} finished worktrees can be cleaned up" per repository when its list changes; `lanes::briefing` is ready for the launch wizard. UI done: worktrees nest under their repository in the Code rail ("New worktree…" in a repository's menu; off with "Worktrees need a git repository" for plain folders) and in the Dashboard's project grouping; a worktree row and the pane ⋯ menu offer Open diff (DiffView in `@tandem/ui`), Merge into {base}… (conflicts listed, nothing committed), Create pull request and Discard worktree… (typed branch-name confirm, then the native confirm when there's uncommitted work); panes in a worktree show its branch chip. A merge without a git identity now says how to set one instead of git's raw error. E2E-M6-4 (`m6-4-worktrees.e2e.ts`) covers AC1–4 on a real repository; the Conductor-origin half of AC2 stays on `invariant_rule_5_local_review_origin` until Conductor exists (M10).
- [x] 3. Launch wizard and presets F-CD-14 (UI-12). Presets: built-ins Solo, Pair, Bench and Fleet plus saved ones in `presets` (`presets_list` / `preset_save` / `preset_delete`; names unique, 1–8 seats). `launch_lineup` checks the whole lineup against the live-pane limit before anything starts, creates the worktrees first when each agent gets its own (one failure doesn't undo the others), puts every pane in its worktree's tab or one shared tab named after the task, and types each agent's first prompt (briefing, role brief, task) once its terminal is live; shell seats run their command once the shell is ready. `launch_retry_seat` relaunches one failed seat. The Launch agents sheet (Mod+Shift+L, the palette, "Launch several…" in an empty tab) has the preset picker, saved presets, the lineup (engine or shell command, role, count), the starting prompt, "Give each agent its own worktree" (off with the reason outside a repository; on by default for more than one agent), a summary naming the branches, the failed seats with Retry, Save as preset… and a loading state until presets and engines are known. Found and fixed on the way: the five commands were missing from the capability allowlist (a new test checks every registered command), and selects, menus and tooltips opened behind any sheet or dialog (D-109). E2E-M6-1…3 (`m6-1-launch.e2e.ts`); the E2E reset now removes only top-level workspaces, since a repository takes its worktrees with it.
- [x] 4. Handoff F-CD-16 (UI-13). "Hand off…" in a pane's ⋯ menu and "Hand off to…" on Dashboard cards open "Hand off to another agent": target engine (another installed engine by default, Claude Code first), Where (same folder or worktree, or a new worktree), the summary written on open with "Writing summary…" and editable, "Close the original pane after handoff", Start {target}. `handoff_brief` tries the three strategies in order: the live source agent is asked for a brief between markers (`tandem-engines::handoff`), else the target engine runs headless over the session's last 400 redacted lines on stdin, else the fallback puts the last 200 lines under the template's headings. `handoff_start` opens the target split right of the source (or in the new worktree's tab) and types the brief once the agent is live; briefs over 8 KB go through `$DATA/briefings/<pane>.md`. The source pane shows "Handed off to {name} ({engine})" under its header until dismissed. `tandem-pty` renders scrollback as plain text for this (`plain_tail`). Found on the way: a headless run inherited the app's own environment; it now gets exactly the sanitized login env and the account's home. E2E-M6-5 (`m6-5-handoff.e2e.ts`) covers AC1 and AC3 with fake Codex and Claude; AC2 (a 200 KB brief) is unit-tested on stdin and the briefing file, since this Linux build can't exercise the Windows argv limit.
- [x] 5. Accounts F-AC-02 (UI-35/106). Settings › Accounts lists engine accounts by engine with nickname (Rename), Default badge, sign-in state (Signed in / Signed out / Checking…), "Usage not reported" until F-AC-04, Make default, Sign in again, Sign out and Remove account… (danger confirm; deletes the provider home). Add account opens a sheet: engine, nickname, then the engine's own login runs in a terminal inside the sheet under a new provider home ("Vibezeo never sees your token"); success is the engine's status command or its sign-in file's presence, never the token; Cancel kills the login and leaves no account and no home; Free gets "Pro lets you add more accounts per engine." with Upgrade. `accounts_begin_add` / `accounts_begin_session` / `accounts_finish_login` / `accounts_cancel_login` / `accounts_update` / `accounts_set_default` / `accounts_remove` / `accounts_status` / `accounts_overrides`, event `accounts:login-finished`. E2E-M6-6 (`m6-6-accounts.e2e.ts`).
- [x] 6. Account resolution F-AC-03. `tandem_core::accounts::resolve` (explicit > teammate > workspace override > default, unit-tested with the milestone's B/C case); a new agent pane is pinned to the account it resolves to; the AccountChip on agent panes shows it and sets the workspace override for new panes (`workspace_set_account_override`); Settings › Accounts lists overrides with ×.

Resume at reset types "continue" at the reset by the app's Clock; while scheduled the banner reads "Paused · resumes …" with Resume now and Cancel auto-resume. Nothing switches account without a click. `limits_list`, `limit_resume_at_reset`, `limit_cancel_resume`, `limit_resume_now`, `limit_dismiss`, `limit_continue`; events `limit:hit`, `limit:changed`. - [x] 8. Usage F-AC-04. `tandem_engines::usage` reads Codex's windows through `codex app-server` (`initialize` → `account/rateLimits/read`, JSON-RPC lines on stdio, `parse_rate_limits` unit-tested; a stand-in app-server tests the probe). `usage_get` / `usage_refresh` (`UsageBoard`, `usage:changed`) give one `UsageView` per account; engines without a source are "unavailable" with the reason (D-113). The title-bar chip (UsageChip) shows the focused agent pane's account, or the default engine's default account: "Codex · 85% · resets 4:10 PM" for the window closest to exhaustion, `warning` from 80% and `danger` from 95%, two xs meters from 1536 px (D-113), "Usage unavailable" with the reason as its tooltip; the popover lists every account's windows with Refresh and Manage accounts…. The webview refreshes every 5 min and after an agent's turn (30 s per account). The fake Codex answers `app-server` from `TANDEM_FAKE_USAGE`.
- [x] 9. Context meter F-AC-06. `tandem_engines::transcript::context_fill` reads the last `usage` in a Claude transcript inside the provider home (tests: 150K of 200K, the 1M window, a path or link outside the home is never read). The status service hands each Stop hook's `transcript_path` to the app, which publishes `context:changed` (`ContextBoard`, `contexts_list`; cleared when the pane goes). `ContextMeter` in `packages/ui` (ring plus "150K / 200K", `warning` from 80%, the spec's tooltip) sits in the agent pane header.
